R22.4 How to use this notebook in the last month

  1. Week 1–2: read the revision card and fact bank of every unit; answer each self-test aloud.
  2. Week 3: write all model-answer questions within the word limit, then compare with the model answers.
  3. Week 4: revise from this unit's chronology and personality tables, plus the traps list of every unit.

4What historians say

The standard reference works for the whole syllabus are G.N. Sharma, Rajasthan ka Itihas and Social Life in Medieval Rajasthan; Dasharath Sharma (ed.), Rajasthan Through the Ages; G.H. Ojha's state histories; Rima Hooja, A History of Rajasthan; and the Rajasthan Board of Secondary Education textbooks. Each unit's "What historians say" section explains where they disagree.

5Prelims fact bank

  • Gokulbhai Bhatt is called the "Gandhi of Rajasthan"; Bhogilal Pandya the "Gandhi of Vagad".
  • Vijay Singh Pathik's real name was Bhoop Singh.
  • Hiralal Shastri founded Banasthali Vidyapith (with Ratan Shastri).
  • Manikyalal Verma was prime minister of the United State of Rajasthan (1948).
  • Hiralal Shastri was the first prime minister of Greater Rajasthan (1949).
  • Haribhau Upadhyay was chief minister of Ajmer state (1952–56).
  • Chandrasen is called the "Pratap of Marwar".
  • Chunda of Mewar is called the "Bhishma of Mewar".
  • Suraj Mal is often called the "Plato of the Jats".
  • Kishori Devi led the Katrathal women's conference (1934).

Exam traps

  • Two "Gandhis": Gokulbhai Bhatt (Rajasthan) and Bhogilal Pandya (Vagad).
  • Two Prithvirajs: Chauhan (Tarain) and Rathore (poet, Bikaner).
  • Two Hammirs: Hammir Dev Chauhan (Ranthambhor) and Rana Hammir (Mewar).
  • Two Jai Singhs of Amber: Mirza Raja (Purandar) and Sawai (Jaipur).
  • Two Man Singhs: Man Singh I of Amber (Mughal general) and Man Singh of Jodhpur (Nath patron; 1818 treaty).

6Previous-year questions (RAS)

Personality-based PYQs appear in the relevant units (e.g. Dungji–Jawaharji in R15, Mewar Pukar in R18). The history sections of the RAS Prelims 2021, 2023 and 2024 papers had no question on this unit (checked against RPSC final answer keys; 2018 not checked, key unavailable).

7Mains answer writing

2 marks · 15 words Who was Vijay Singh Pathik?

How to frame it

  • Identity
  • Contribution
Model answer (15 words)

Revolutionary (born Bhoop Singh) who led the Bijolia movement and founded the Rajasthan Seva Sangh.

2 marks · 15 words Who was Manikyalal Verma?

How to frame it

  • Identity
  • Contribution
Model answer (16 words)

Founder of the Mewar Praja Mandal (1938); first prime minister of the United State of Rajasthan.

2 marks · 15 words Who was Gokulbhai Bhatt?

How to frame it

  • Identity
  • Contribution
Model answer (13 words)

Sirohi Praja Mandal leader, "Gandhi of Rajasthan", who opposed separating Abu from Rajasthan.

8One-screen revision card

  • Medieval: Kumbha, Sanga, Pratap (Mewar); Jodha, Maldeo, Chandrasen, Durgadas (Marwar); Man Singh I, Sawai Jai Singh (Amber); Suraj Mal (Bharatpur); Zalim Singh (Kota).
  • Freedom: Pathik, Ramnarayan Chaudhary, Haribhau Upadhyay, Barhaths, Sethi, Kharwa, Gopa; Govind Guru, Tejawat.
  • Praja Mandal and integration: Bajaj, Hiralal Shastri, Manikyalal Verma, Jai Narayan Vyas, Gokulbhai Bhatt, Bhogilal Pandya.
  • Chronology anchors: 1192, 1303, 1459, 1527, 1568, 1576, 1615, 1727, 1818, 1857, 1913, 1938, 1942, 1949, 1956.

9Self-test

  1. Who is called the "Gandhi of Rajasthan"?

    Answer

    Gokulbhai Bhatt.

  2. Who is called the "Gandhi of Vagad"?

    Answer

    Bhogilal Pandya.

  3. Who was the first prime minister of Greater Rajasthan?

    Answer

    Hiralal Shastri.

  4. Who founded Banasthali Vidyapith?

    Answer

    Hiralal and Ratan Shastri.

  5. Who led the Rathore war for Ajit Singh?

    Answer

    Durgadas Rathore.

  6. Who wrote Vamsha Bhaskar?

    Answer

    Suryamal Mishran.

  7. Who sponsored the Child Marriage Restraint Act (1929)?

    Answer

    Har Bilas Sarda.

  8. Which ruler founded Jaipur?

    Answer

    Sawai Jai Singh II (1727).

  9. Who led the Katrathal women's conference?

    Answer

    Kishori Devi.
